# Admin_Features


## Fleet Expiration Reset

`Admin_Features/FleetExpireReset.htm`

You are here: Title & Registration Services > Vehicle Management > Fleet Expiration
Reset
Fleet Expiration Reset
This functionality allows the user to reset the Expiration Date of a Fleet to one year prior so a Fleet
Renewal may be restarted.
Example: Customers with large fleets will sometimes wait until after hours (e.g. 9 pm) to begin
a Fleet Renewal. Unfortunately, the renewal takes several hours and overlaps when the MAX
Cash Drawers are reset at midnight, therefore the renewal does not complete.
Who Can Perform this Function
Using the Fleet Expiration Reset

## Receipt Search

`Admin_Features/Receipt_Search.htm`

You are here: Finance & Cash Management > Receipt Search
Receipt Search
This function allows you to download a specific payment receipt, and requires you to already have the
Receipt Number.
You see a payment on a report that you want to research in more detail. You use the Receipt
Number (Receipt ID) from the report in the Receipt Search function to download a copy of that
payment receipt.
1. From the Admin menu, click Receipt Search.
2. Type or paste the Receipt ID.
3. Click Download.
4. The downloaded receipt now appears in your Downloads folder on your local machine.
 
HELPFUL LINKS
MVD / DMV Public Website
 
 
 
 
 
Customer Portal

## Service History Lookup

`Admin_Features/Service_History_Lookup.htm`

You are here: About Services in MAX > Service History Lookup
Service History Lookup
Some MVD units may need to look up a completed service by the Business Transaction Number (aka:
Service ID) rather than by the customer's Service History.
1. From the Admin Menu Select Service History Lookup.
2. Type the Business Transaction Number (aka: Service ID) related to the service you'd like to view.
3. Click Find.
4. The Service Artifacts for the service you requested will display. From here you can download and view the
Service Artifacts and documents that were added during the service.

# ATP_Audits


## Daily Quality Assurance Process: ATP Self QC

`ATP_Audits/ATP_Self_QC.htm`

You are here: Authorized Third Party Offices > ATP Audit Processes > Daily Quality
Assurance Process: ATP Self QC
Daily Quality Assurance Process: ATP Self QC
Initially, ATP Point of Contacts (POCs) are the only individuals who have access to the QC folders.
POCs may disperse folder access to other branch managers, as needed.
If a POC changes, ATPs must notify the Certification Desk at certificationdesk@azdot.gov
If notification is not received, you will not be able to open your folder
The MAX report will be added on a daily basis. ATPs will need to monitor their 3rd Party Self QC Google
spreadsheet and quality check their work daily.
If a finding(s) 'notated item' are found, ATPs will have seven days to correct it.
If not corrected, it will be added to the Compliance Inspection Report as a notated finding upon
the pre-inspection process
This Google Shared Drive and and any files transmitted within are confidential and solely
intended for the use of the individual or entity to whom they are assigned.
You should not disseminate, distribute, or copy confidential content.
You are notified that disclosing, copying, distributing, or taking any action in reliance on the
contents of this information is strictly prohibited and can result in a corrective action.
QC Process
QC Findings
No Findings (Notated Items)
Findings (Notated Items)
QC is Complete
Additional References
MVD One Source > ATP Content Only folder > ATP Audit Processes
Google Classroom > TPA Training Academy > Training Resources

## Auditing an ARO

`ATP_Audits/Auditing_an_ARO.htm`

You are here: Authorized Third Party Offices > ATP Audit Processes > Auditing an
ARO
Auditing an ARO
The information provided below is how the Compliance - Quality Assurance Unit audits specific
information.
Items Needed for Auditing
Procedures:
Arizona Registration Only (ARO)/ Special Interstate Registration (SPI)
Policies:
8.10.4 Arizona Registration Only (ARO)/Government Registration Only (GRO)
8.15.5 Establishing a Factory List Price for Commercial Vehicles (Over 18,000 Lbs GVWR)
Charts:
Converting O/S Legal Status to Arizona
World Data Corp (Polk Book) Quick Reference Guide
Example of Acceptable Documentation
Auditing an ARO
1. Verify whether state of origin is or is not a title holding state from the Polk Book.
2. Verify the legal status from the Converting O/S Legal Status chart.
3. Verify the customer names in MAX match the registration document and, if applicable, the names
have been added to the AKAs in the customer record.
4. Follow the Arizona Registration Only (ARO)/ Special Interstate Registration (SPI) topic > Additional
Documentation (If Approved) section and verify approval was granted for the additional
documentation.
One signature is acceptable regardless of the legal status, per policy 8.10.4(C)(1).
ARO Processing Notes

Proof of ownership cannot be a sales contract, drive-out permit, or a temporary registration.

## Auditing an OHV Decal

`ATP_Audits/Auditing_an_OHV_Decal.htm`

You are here: Authorized Third Party Offices > ATP Audit Processes > Auditing an OHV
Decal
Auditing an OHV Decal
The information provided below is how the Compliance - Quality Assurance Unit audits specific information.
Items Needed for Auditing
Procedures:
Off-Highway Vehicle (OHV) Decal
Off-Highway Vehicle (OHV) Decal topic > Off-Highway Vehicle (OHV) Decal section
Policies:
8.13.4 Off-Highway Vehicle
Charts:
OHV Registration Chart
Forms:
Off-Highway Vehicle Decal Application, form #96-0572
Auditing an OHV Decal
1. Verify the VIN on the decal application matches the vehicle's record in MAX.
That is, if it's applicable if title work was completed

2. Verify the title information any supporting documentation that may have been submitted and the
vehicle's record in MAX.
That is, if title work was completed
3. Verify the owner's names and address(es) matches the decal application and title work (if applicable);
as well as ensure the proper ownership chain was followed.
4. Verify the lienholder(s) and lien date(s) matches any supporting documentation that may have been
submitted and the vehicle's record in MAX, if applicable.

5. Verify the information on the OHV Decal application to the vehicle's record in MAX and to how the
vehicle was registered.
6. Verify all documents were properly scanned. View the Document Management topic on how to view
documents in the customer's record.

ATP Company Details
UNDER CONSTRUCTION
ATP Management and Case Management (Manage Case) is still a work in progress, as well as the
topics provided to you in this folder.
Please be patient as we continuously improve the content.
ATP Management in MAX consists of ATP Company. Only main branches of the ATP will have access to the
ATP Company service.
The ATP Company details screen provides all organizational information:
Summary Tab
Contacts Tab
Locations Tab
New locations require a new case
For additional information, see the Create New Location Application topic
Employees Tab 
New employees require a new case
For additional information, see the Create New Employee Application
Bonds/Insurance Tab
Agreements Tab
Inspections Tab
Documents Tab
Notes Tab
Each tab within the ATP Company's details screen is broken down into their own topic within this folder.
These topics provide information on how to make updates to the ATP.
ATPs have the ability to add or edit certain organizational information in MAX
After being added or edited, certain information will be reviewed and approved by TPA
Additional requirements or documents may be requested by TPA through MAX notification
system

2. The company's information will display.

## Summary Tab

`ATP_Content_ONLY/ATP_Mgmt/1_SummaryTab.htm`

You are here: Authorized Third Party Offices > ATP Management - Third Party Offices >
ATP Company Details > Summary Tab
Summary Tab
The Summary tab is the first tab within the ATP Company details screen. This tab provides basic info on the
ATP.
1. Customer Name: ATP's name.
2. View DBA Names: Click the blue down arrow to view a list of the ATP's associated names.
3. Customer Details:
Customer Type (organization)
Customer Number (unique identifier assigned by MAX)
EIN (Employer Identification Number)
4. Customer Address: ATP's business location and/or mailing addresses.
5. Financial Information: 
Bank Name
TPPP ID: Third Party Payment Portal; Used to track payments

Office Deposit Code: Business Unit
Commercial Crime/Fidelity Minimum - depends on number of locations and services provided
$50,000; $150,000; $250,000; $750,000; $2,000,000
Bond Minimum
T&R services - $100,000 per location
DL services - $300,000 per location
1099 Eligible: IRS form submitted for the ATPs retention fees.
Used by Finance Unit. Only internal ADOT units may edit the field
6. UNDER CONSTRUCTION Statutory Agent.
Updates to Customer Name
If you want to update your ATP's company name, you would need to Create New ATP Application.
Updates to Customer Details
The Customer Number (MAX Customer Number) cannot be changed.
If you are changing the EIN, this is the same as a Customer Name change, and you would need to Create
New ATP Application.
Updates to Customer Address
If you are updating an address that does not affect the ATP's location:
1. Complete the Third Party Change Notification, form #96-0518.
2. Upload the form in the Documents tab.
Document Category: ATP Employee
Document Type: Employee Documents
For additional information, see the Documents Tab on how to upload documents
3. Email the CertificationDesk@azdot.gov.

Customer Portal

Bonds/Insurance Tab
Insurance
All ATPs are required to have one of each of the following Insurance Types, along with the required
documentation for each type. The amount of coverage depends on the Insurance Type. Some ATPs
may choose to insurer themselves for more than the required minimum.
The length of time an insurance policy is valid depends on the type of insurance.
Insurance Types and Required Documents
Commercial General Liability
Declarations Page or Certificate(s) of insurance - Accord form
Commercial general liability - additional insured endorsement form for ongoing operations
Commercial general liability - waiver of subrogation endorsement form
Commercial general liability - primary and noncontributory endorsement form
Auto in Commercial General Liability
Hired and non-owned auto (covered in the GL)
Auto Liability
Declarations Page or Certificate(s) of insurance - Accord form
Auto liability - additional insured endorsement form
Auto liability - waiver of subrogation endorsement form
Umbrella (optional)
Certificate of Insurance (COI)
Workers Compensation
Declarations Page or Certificate(s) of insurance - Accord form
Worker’s compensation - waiver of subrogation endorsement form
Cyber Liability
Declarations Page or Certificate(s) of insurance - Accord form
Cyber Liability - additional insured endorsement form for ongoing operations
Cyber Liability - waiver of subrogation endorsement form

Cyber Liability - primary and noncontributory endorsement form
Crime/Fidelity
Fidelity or employee dishonesty policy declarations page(s)
Crime/fidelity - computer fraud
Crime/fidelity - forgery and/or alteration
Crime/fidelity - employee dishonesty
Crime/fidelity - inside/outside premises
If applicable: include the policy declaration pages (insuring agreements) with
verbiage for the above required limits
Crime/fidelity - loss payee endorsement forms
Accessing & Reading Insurance Details
Add Insurance
Bonds
All ATPs are required to have bonds, depending on what services that are provided.
T&R - $100,000 per location
DL - $300,000 per company
Inspection Only
Bonds are valid for one year.
ATPs can purchase a bond through any insurance company that issues bonds.
Exempt Bonds
If the ATP qualifies to have the bond requirement exempted, the following document is required:
Third Party Bond Requirement Exemption, form #96-0517
Accessing & Reading Bond Details

## Agreements Tab

`ATP_Content_ONLY/ATP_Mgmt/6_AgreementsTab.htm`

You are here: Authorized Third Party Offices > ATP Management - Third Party
Offices > ATP Company Details > Agreements Tab
Agreements Tab
Agreements are needed between ADOT and the ATP prior to opening to grant authorization to perform
authorized services.
An Agreement term is for a period up to 12 months. Therefore, these agreements will needed to
be amended and/or renewed
A Joint Letter of Rnewal (JLOR) can be issued to the extend the agreement for an additional one
year, to a total of four JLORs
After a total of five years, the agreement cycle process starts over with a new full agreement
Agreement amendments and/or addedendums will be issued as needed
Agreements, amendments, or addendums will be sent through DocuSign for review, approval, and
signatures, which is sent by TPA. Once received, TPA will add the agreement(s) to MAX. The
Agreements tab will be used to Acknowledge the agreement(s).
Agreement Types Descriptions
Agreement and Exhibits
Full agreement with Exhibits
Joint Letter of Renewal (JLOR)
Agreement Renewal for one year
Agreement Amendment
Amendment of Full Agreement, used for relocation of primary place of business (main
location)
Agreement Addendum (not currently used)
Used to add something to the agreement
Agreement Exhibit Amendments
Used for changes to an Exhibit
Agreement Exhibit Addendums
Used for adding something to an Exhibit

Exhibit Types Descriptions
Exhibit A - Title & Registration Authorized Activities
Exhibit B - Only for Companies with Branch locations
Exhibit C - Insurance and Bonding Requirements
Exhibit D - Performance Measures
Exhibit E - Non-Discrimination
Accessing and Reading Agreement Details
Edit Agreement: Acknowledged Agreements

## Inspections Tab

`ATP_Content_ONLY/ATP_Mgmt/7_InspectionsTab.htm`

You are here: Authorized Third Party Offices > ATP Management - Third Party Offices >
ATP Company Details > Inspections Tab
Inspections Tab
The Third Party Compliance Program will conduct an Initial Title and Registration (T&R) and/or Driver License
(DL) inspection to ensure compliance with contractual agreement and applicable Arizona Department of
Transportation Motor Vehicle Department (ADOT MVD) policies and procedures, which are designed to
safeguard public monies, accountable forms and to promote public safety.
Prior to the inspection, the Third Party Compliance Program will send a DocuSign of the Engagement Letter,
along with a PDF version and the Bill of Rights. After the DocuSign has been completed, the auditor will add
the inspection to the Inspection tab.
The Third Party Compliance Auditor will communicate the result(s) of any non-compliance findings to the ATP
owner and or management at the end of the inspection. A finalized compliance report will be completed within
30 days of the inspection.
The Inspection tab will also be updated to display the Inspection Results.
Reading the Inspection Tab
1. Inspection Type.
Announced
Follow-Up
Management Requested
Office Closure
2. Services.
3. Auditor.
4. Inspection Date.
5. Inspection Results.
Critical Finding(s)

Non-Critical Finding(s)
Compliant
6. Status.
Inspection Scheduled
Inspection Pending
Onsite Inspection Conducted
Inspection Report Generated/Complete
Inspection Completed

## Create New Location Application

`ATP_Content_ONLY/ATP_Mgmt/ATP_Case_Mgmt-New_Branch_App.htm`

You are here: Case Management > AZ - ATP Applications > Create New Location
Application
Create New Location Application
This topic will be used when an ATP wants to apply for a new branch of their ATP.
Overall Process
1. ATP will Create New Case - New Location Application (Candidate Phase).
2. TPA begins Preliminary Review Phase.
3. TPA begins Interest Phase and ATP provides additional required documentation and complete newly
available fields in the case.
4. TPA continues review of application (Formal Phase (ATP will complete newly available fields in the
case), Canceled Phase, Closed Phase , as applicable).
Notifications
As cases are worked by TPA, most notifications about the case will be sent through the Internal Office
Notifications system. Other notifications may still be completed through emails.
Required Documentation
For the Candidate Phase:
Business Plan
Training Plan
For the Interest Phase:
Certificate of Occupancy
Pre-Approval of Surety Bond
Menu Fee Board
Computer Equipment Request
Floor Plan
Master Information Sheet (MIS)
Marketing Plan
Lease

Additional documentation may be required as the Case is processed.
Application Status and Reason Chart
The Status and Status Reason combination in the case generally manage the flow of the process, as
they are updated, new information in the case is made available to complete in that phase of the case
review.
The following lists the various Status and Status Reason combinations and how they impact the case
flow.
Not all Statuses will require the ATP to complete something within the ATP Location
Application, most will be completed by the Third Party Administration (TPA).
ATP Location Application Statuses and Reasons
Candidate Phase
Create New Case - New Location Application
Candidate Phase - Continued
Preliminary Review
Interest Phase
New
Requirements Needed
Research
Formal Phase
New
Ready for Processing
Requirements Needed
Pending
Canceled Phase
Application Withdrawn
Closed Phase
Application Approved
Rejected: Preliminary, Interest, Formal

ATP Company
ATP Management in MAX consists of the ATP Company service.

Add new locations or apply to offer more services

View uploaded information from TPA (example: inspection results)

employees, or etc.

# AZ_MVD_Now


## Assisted Self-Service Invitations (ASI)

`AZ_MVD_Now/Assisted_Self-Service.htm`

You are here: Customer Portal > Assisted Self-Service Invitations (ASI)
Assisted Self-Service Invitations (ASI)
Assisted Self-Service Invitations (ASI) provide the customer an opportunity to upload documents or fund their
account through AZ MVD Now without completing the full AZ MVD Now Account Setup and authentication
processes.
After verifying the customer's identity over the phone, the CSR can send the customer a limited-time invitation
link by email or text. The customer will use the link and code within the email or text (temporary username) to
access a restricted guest version of AZ MVD Now, and will use a 4-digit PIN provided over the phone by the
CSR to access their account for the specified purpose (Fund Account, Upload Documents, schedule an
appointment (PIN not required) or eSign Document).
If the customer does not use the link within the indicated time period, the invitation will expire.
The person who receives the email or text invitation may or may not be the customer themselves
(e.g. a Parent funds an account for the Child). You will add the Invitation to the Customer Record
where the funds or documents will reside, regardless of who receives the email or text.
In the case of eSign, regardless of who receives the invitation, the signer of the document
(Customer) must verify the Last Name, Date of Birth, and Last 4 digits of SSN in order to eSign the
document.
Adding Self-Service Invitation
Adding a Document eSign Invitation

Message Templates

Customer Survey (Contact Center ONLY)
Sending ASI for OHV Education Course to Knowledge Testing Station

Video

## Basic Troubleshooting for AZ MVD Now

`AZ_MVD_Now/Basic_Troubleshooting.htm`

You are here: Customer Portal > Basic Troubleshooting for AZ MVD Now
Basic Troubleshooting for AZ MVD Now
Your customer is calling because they cannot access their account on AZ MVD Now. This topic will walk
you through basic troubleshooting that will help the customer gain access to their account.
Here is a printable Quick Reference Guide for:
Contact Center
Field or ATP offices
Email Addresses Associated with Customers
Before you begin the troubleshooting steps, it is important to understand that there are three areas in
MAX where you can find an email address for a customer. Each of these email addresses can be
different, has a different purpose, and can be managed in different areas throughout AZ MVD Now.
Never provide an email address on record to the customer.
Email addresses must always be provided by the customer and then you may visually
compare with what is on record.
Contact Email Address
Fund Account Email Address
AZ MVD Now Email Address
Troubleshooting
The following steps are intended to be done in order to identify and correct issues the customer may be
experiencing.
There may be instances where the steps may be done out of order, such as the customer already
knows they need their phone number updated, etc. However, Step 10 and Step 11 have prerequisites
that must be completed first.
Step One: Verify the Customer
Step Two: How is the customer signing in: Google or Email?

Step Three: Does the customer have access to the email address and phone
number associated with their AZ MVD Now Account?
Step Four: Is the customer using a mobile device or computer?
Step Five: What browser is the customer using (Google Chrome, Safari,
DuckDuckGo, etc.)?
Step Six: How strong is the customer's Wi-Fi or Mobile Data signal?
Step Seven: Is the customer partially signed in?
Step Eight: Clear Cache and Browsing History or Open Incognito Window
Step Nine: complete On Demand Authentication
Per OIG, over the phone, Step 9 must be completed before we can unlink a customer's
account.
Step Ten: Unlink AZ MVD Now Account
Per OIG, over the phone, Step 9 must be completed before we can unlink or link a
customer's account and we must only link an email address that we previously unlinked.
Step Eleven: Link AZ MVD Now Account
Step Twelve: Service Portal Ticket

Customer Portal

## Driver License Application in AZ MVD Now

`AZ_MVD_Now/Credential_Application_AZMVDNow.htm`

You are here: Customer Portal > Driver License Application in AZ MVD Now
Driver License Application in AZ MVD Now
This topic provides step by step screens for the customer experience in AZ MVD Now related to Credential
Applications.
1. In AZ MVD Now, from the Driver License & ID menu, click Driver License Application.
2. Helpful information regarding the application process is provided on this screen.
3. Arizona Travel ID information and cost is displayed.
4. Motorcycle License information is displayed. Note: Motorcycle license applications are not yet available
in AZ MVD Now.
5. Click Start Application.

6. Review and confirm Personal Information.
7. Review and confirm Contact Information. If changes are needed, click Edit.
8. Select or type the required Identifying Information.
9. Click Continue.

10. Click the option for the Driver License Application:
Arizona Travel ID (Driver License)
Non-Travel Driver License (NOT FOR FEDERAL IDENTIFICATION)
11. Click Continue.

## Customers Signing Up for AZ MVD Now - Individual Login

`AZ_MVD_Now/Customers_Signing_Up_for_AMN.htm`

You are here: Customer Portal > Customers Signing Up for AZ MVD Now - Individual
Login
Customers Signing Up for AZ MVD Now - Individual Login
For a customer to set up their AZ MVD Now login, they must meet the Customer Account Requirements
(Level 3 Customer), and provide a Social Security Number (SSN) as identification. AZ MVD Now will
attempt to validate against an existing Customer Record, but new customers who have never done
business with MVD before will still be able to create an account.
Even if a customer is a Level 3 customer without an SSN, they must have an SSN to activate
their AZ MVD Now login.
If an existing customer who has not previously provided a Social Security Number attempts to
sign up through AZ MVD Now, they will be prompted to add their Social Security Number before
continuing.

## eTitle Transfer

`AZ_MVD_Now/eTitle_Transfer-Step_by_Step.htm`

You are here: Customer Portal > eTitle Transfer
eTitle Transfer
Individuals may electronically transfer ownership of an eligible passenger vehicle to another individual on AZ
MVD Now, if the vehicle or ownership is eligible.
The Division, paired with Intellectual Technology Inc (ITI), will mail the physical license plate, validation tab,
and registration to the customer.
The 5 Step eTitle Transfer Process
The whole process will be completed in AZ MVD Now. It is a five step process that is completed between the
seller and buyer.
View the Title Menu > eTitle section for full steps to be completed on AZ MVD Now.
1. Buyer Generates eTitle Buyer Code.
2. Seller Shares Vehicle Information.
3. Buyer Reviews Vehicle.
4. Seller Releases Ownership.
5. Buyer Registers Vehicle.

## Managing AZ MVD Now Logins for Organizations

`AZ_MVD_Now/Managing_AZ_MVD_Now_Logins_for_Organizations.htm`

You are here: Customer Portal > Managing AZ MVD Now Logins for Organizations
Managing AZ MVD Now Logins for Organizations
This function in MAX is limited to the Account Support Unit (for all Organizations) or the
Dealer Licensing Unit (for Dealerships).
If Organizations inquire about establishing their Organization Customer Accounts through
AZ MVD Now, CSRs should direct the customer to the appropriate unit:
Dealers should be directed to the Dealer Licensing Unit
All other organizations should be directed to the Account Support Unit
The Central Issuance Mailing Dashboard > Processing Dates tab provides the current date
applications are being worked.
Organizations (including trusts, governments, non-profit organizations, and sole proprietorships) must
meet Customer Account Requirements (Level 3 Customer) to have AZ MVD Now access to their
account. Organizations must also complete an AZ MVD Now Organization Administrator Application,
form #15-0706 to identify the Organization Administrator who will manage access to the Organization
Account through AZ MVD Now.

There are actually two different forms, depending on the type of Organization requesting access
to their account online:
AZ MVD Now Dealership Administrator Application, form #15-0707 (managed by the
Dealer Licensing Unit, aacc@azdot.gov)
AZ MVD Now Organization Administrator Application, form #15-0706 (managed by the
Account Support Unit, accountsupport@azdot.gov) - please note: This is an application for
an account, not a guarantee of account access. After proper due diligence, the
Organization Customer Account will be created by the Account Support Unit, and the
Organization Administrator will be notified of their access
This information is provided from the Activate as an Organization option from the Sign In
& Activation Options drop down menu in AZ MVD Now.
To create a login (username and password) for AZ MVD Now access to the Organization's Customer
Account, Representatives must be added to the Organization Customer Record in MAX.
If the Representative is set up as an Organization Administrator, that individual is granted access to
Manage Representatives directly in AZ MVD Now for the Organization.

Adding or editing Representatives does not create customer records for those individuals, and
those individuals are not subject to the same Customer Account Requirements (Level 3
Customer).
In many cases, you will not be able to add Representatives until the appropriate Characteristic
has been added. See the Characteristics for more information about Characteristics for
Organization Records.
Updating an Account Administrator
If the organization needs to update the account administrator, they must go through the process for
application again so that Account Support can make the necessary changes.
Policies
4.1.4 Customer AZMVDNow.gov Access
Forms
#15-0706 AZ MVD Now Organization Administrator Application

## Mobile ID

`AZ_MVD_Now/Mobile_ID.htm`

You are here: Driver & Event Management > Arizona ID/DL in Mobile Wallets > Mobile
ID
Mobile ID
Mobile ID (mID) is a voluntary, secure, digital version of your ADOT MVD-issued DL or ID that is carried
in an app on a smartphone. mID carries the same legal weight and backing as the physical driver
license and can be used as proof of identity in-person and online. See Arizona ID in Mobile Wallets topic
for additional information.
Mobile ID will not display 'limited term' wording for individuals with a limited credential.
Expired credentials will not appear in the app.
Mobile ID can be used for:
Certain services that require enhanced authentication on AZ MVD Now to verify the user's identity
In-person when you need to provide proof of identity (e.g., in a MVD/ATP office)
Until Mobile ID acceptance grows, a physical ID should still be carried.
mID is not available for customers with the ACP Characteristic.
Direct customers to mobileID.az.gov for additional information regarding mID, including:
Additional information on why it was created
A 'How to Activate' video
A 'How to Verify' video

## Prepaid Voucher

`AZ_MVD_Now/Prepaid_Voucher.htm`

You are here: Finance & Cash Management > Prepaid Voucher
Prepaid Voucher
Only AZ MVD Now Organization Accounts are able to purchase PPVs and they can only do so
using their AZ MVD Now account. However, they can give them to anyone to use to pay for an
MVD service in an MVD Field Office or AZ MVD Now.
A Prepaid Voucher (PPV) from an Organization customer who has an AZ MVD Now account and they
have deposited money into their account and has produced the PPV.
Are redeemable for any Division service
Printable from any printer
The printed copy of the PPV is not necessary as long as the Organization customer knows
the PPV number
Will have a unique identifying number (PPV number)
Usable immediately, including the day of issuance
Similar to a 'gift card' and allows the holder of the PPV to give either the printed PPV or the PPV
number to anyone, such as but not limited to, a family member or friend to pay for services
rendered with the Division
No more than four PPVs may be used to pay for any one service and may be combined with any
other form of payment (e.g., another PPV, check, or cash, etc.)
Not renewable, reloadable, or refundable

## Request Customer Disbursement

`AZ_MVD_Now/Request_Customer_Disbursement.htm`

You are here: Finance & Cash Management > Request Customer Disbursement
Request Customer Disbursement
If a customer has an unused balance on a Prepaid Voucher or a balance in their Customer Account,
those funds can be returned to them using the Disbursement Request feature.
The customer has the option of either receiving a check, or to have the disbursement credited to their
bank account (this option only available when submitted by customer).
Organizations will only be able to receive Check Disbursements, unless they have the Trusted
Organization Characteristic on their Customer Record. See Trusted Organization for more information.
Check disbursements are usually received within 7-10 days.
A Disbursement Request can only be made 7 days after the original account funding as an anti-
fraud measure. If the request is made within 7 days of account funding, you will get an error.
This does not apply to Registration Credits or other MVD refunds. Disbursement requests for
those funds can be made immediately.
If the customer funded their own account, and had no MVD Refunds or Credits, they will only
be able to make one Disbursement Request in a 30-day period. Additional Disbursement
Requests from the account may be made for each MVD Refund or Credit to the account.
Example: a customer received 6 plate credits in one month, so MAX will allow 7
Disbursement Requests (Number of MVD Refunds/Credits + 1)
If the customer contacts the MVD as to why they have not received their disbursement checks that have
already been processed, the customer or CSR can email the Refund Unit at
ADOT_PFTO_Refunds@azdot.gov (preferred method of contact) to inquire, or call at 602-712-7507.

Title Viewer
There are 3 different title views available:
Title Information (Full): An electronic snapshot of the title details found on the front side of a
paper title. You will only see these details if you are listed as an owner on the title
Title Information (Limited): Limited information, but indicators that let a potential buyer know
whether or not the vehicle is eligible for transfer
Share Title Information (Redacted Address): A view of the vehicle title that a vehicle owner can
opt to send to another person. It contains all the title details provided in the full title view, except
the vehicle owner's address

## Voter Registration

You are here: Customer Portal > Voter Registration
Voter Registration
To register to vote in Arizona, you must meet the following qualifications:
Be a citizen of the United States of America
Be a resident of Arizona and the county listed on your registration
Be 18 years of age on or before the day of the next regular General Election
See Registration Requirements on the Arizona Secretary of State website for more details.
Currently, citizens may register to vote when obtaining a credential from the MVD. This happens from
the Questionnaire tab in a Credential Issuance service in MAX. See Voter Registration Chart for more
information.

Arizona Wallet Application
The AZ Wallet is a voluntary, secure, app on a smartphone, which allows you to upload a digital version
of your ADOT MVD-issued DL or ID. Id's uploaded carry the same legal weight as the physical driver
licenses and can be used as proof of identify in-person. This is similar to Mobile Wallets/Mobile ID,
except this is a separate app that will allow other state issued documentation to be uploaded in the
future. See Arizona Wallet topic for additional information.

residence) credential.

In-person when you need to provide proof of identity, ownership, etc.

## Balance Cash Drawer

`Cash Management/Balance_Cash_Drawer.htm`

You are here: Finance & Cash Management > Balance Cash Drawer
Balance Cash Drawer
At the end of their shift the CSR balances their cash drawer, which confirms the amount of the cash
drawer matches the amount the system has recorded as collected for services throughout the day.
The CSR returns the physical Cash Drawer to the Accountant and they count out and confirm ending
cash. The Accountant accepts the Balanced or Unbalanced Cash Drawer back from the CSR. If
unbalanced, the Accountant will work to balance or make necessary adjustments to balance.
If an error is discovered after the CSR has submitted their Balanced or Un-Balanced drawer,
but BEFORE the accountant clicks the Accept button, the CSR can use the Balance Cash
Drawer steps and MAX will allow the starting over of balancing (as if it were the first time). This
will allows the errors to be corrected.

## Cash Pickup

`Cash Management/Cash_Pick_Up.htm`

You are here: Finance & Cash Management > Cash Pickup
Cash Pickup
A Cash Pickup is how you will remove excess cash from your Cash Drawer so the drawer balance is
below the specified amount set for the office. Each time a payment is received in MAX, the system
verifies whether the drawer has reached the amount requiring a Cash Pickup. When the Cash Pickup
threshold is hit, a CSR will receive a notification message to complete a Cash Pickup.

## End of Day Closing

`Cash Management/End_of_Day_Closing.htm`

You are here: Finance & Cash Management > End of Day Closing
End of Day Closing
The Accountant uses the consolidated (all CSR) End of Day Closing view in MAX to ensure all cash
drawers are balanced and accepted. When all drawers are collected and each shows a status of
Accepted - Balanced, the Balance Vault option becomes available.
When completing End of Day accounting processes, all subsequent steps (Deposit Slip, Rollup,
and Balance Vault) will automatically flow in MAX from the End of Day Closing screen.
As a Best Practice, remember to Return any Cash Drawers that were issued in MAX but did
not have any payment activity. You will Return Cash Drawers from the Issue Cash Drawer area
of MAX, and then return to End of Day Closing.

## Payment Processing

`Cash Management/Payment_Processing.htm`

You are here: Finance & Cash Management > Payment Processing
Payment Processing
For field offices using the integrated Point of Sale (POS) Devices: The OOF Business Units
now accept cash, check, credit cards, customer account and PrePaid Vouchers as payment
options. This is now the primary BU when issuing cash drawers.
In MAX, services will be added to a Shopping Cart (checkout feature) similar to what online retailers use for
purchases. This means you can add multiple services to the cart and view a running total of what is contained
in the cart on the Checkout screen.
When a customer has funds in an acceptable account, MAX will automatically apply those funds
first at Checkout towards the amount due.
View the Acceptable Methods of Payment topic for more information on the different types of acceptable
payments.

Changes made while processing a service are only updated in MAX after the associated fee has
been paid and processed during the checkout process. No credentials will print until payment is
finalized.
In certain situations, the system may automatically retain changes made - such as a change of
address - even if the service is not processed.

## Check-In

`Cross_Functional/Check_In.htm`

You are here: About Services in MAX > Check-In
Check-In
In MAX there are several ways to start a service for a customer. One way is "Check-In".
When a greeter uses this method to start a service they are adding them to the Business Unit (BU)
Queue so a CSR can quickly begin providing the service when the customer arrives at their window.
When a CSR uses this method, the customer is added to the BU Queue and is automatically assigned
to the CSR when they click Begin Services.
Using Check-In provides visibility to the following:
Open Services - These are services that were previously put in Work In Progress .
Suggested Services - These are services that will be due in the coming months that the
customer may want to complete during their current visit (such as Registration or Credential
Renewals). This section will also include any appointments the customer has made.
Queued Services - These are services that have begun but have not been placed into Work in
Progress. This section will also include any services the customer has initiated via AZ MVD Now
(such as Credential Applications).
Add Service - Search for the service(s) the customer wants to complete and add them to the list
of services to be added to the BU Queue.

## Document Management

`Cross_Functional/Document_Management.htm`

You are here: Documents and Scanning > Document Management
Document Management
Documents in MAX are supplemental files used to satisfy business rules, prove eligibility, and meet
requirements for customers to complete MAX services. Documents can also include MAX service
artifacts and financial transaction receipts.
All documents that are scanned to the Documents area of the Customer Record are viewable by the
Customer in AZ MVD Now.
Based on configured business rules or privacy levels of the documents, some documents scanned to a
service (view Scanning in MAX) may not be visible in AZ MVD Now.
For a helpful reference on what Document Types can be found under each Document
Category, view the Document Scanning Charts section for more information.
Documents used to satisfy business rules, prove eligibility, and meet service requirements are loaded
into MAX in different ways.
They can be uploaded by the customer through AZ MVD Now.
They may be scanned into MAX by a CSR in the MVD office (generally after a paper version is
supplied by the customer to meet the requirements for the Service they are requesting) . View
Scanning In MAX for more information. Based on configured business rules or privacy levels of
the documents, some documents scanned to a service (view Scanning in MAX) may not be
visible in AZ MVD Now
They can be generated by MAX, such as service artifacts or financial transaction receipts. MAX-
generated documents will also display in the Customer's Documents and cannot be deleted.
Documents that are viewed in OnBase do not necessarily need to be uploaded into MAX. For
Credential Issuance services, do pull the document from OnBase and upload into the service
in MAX to ensure the requirements are satisfied.
 

Original Credentials, including CDLs, have a 10-year retention period (from date of issuance)
for all source documents provided to establish a name and date of birth, including the
Credential Application. All source documents must be scanned/uploaded within the service
when issuing an original or renewal.

## eSignature

`Cross_Functional/eSignature.htm`

You are here: Documents and Scanning > eSignature
eSignature
eSignature allows you to obtain a customer's signature electronically for MVD documents requiring a
signature. For example, if a customer forgets to sign a submitted Title and Registration Application, or
the Title Summary page, instead of mailing the customer the document to sign and return to MVD, use
eSignature to request their signature electronically.
eSignature can be used only on MVD documents that have the 'MVD Agent or Notary' section. If it is a
document that only has a notary option, then a physical signature will be required.
If the customer has already activated their Customer Account they can eSign the document in
AZ MVD Now. Or,
The customer must have an email address in their Customer Record. See Adding an Email
Address to a Customer Account for more information.
AND
The customer must have a Social Security Number (SSN) on the Customer Record in order to
eSign a document. See Adding or Editing a Social Security Number (SSN) for more information.
eSignature Process Overview
The eSignature process consists of three main steps:
1. MVD: The document requiring a signature is scanned or uploaded into the Customer Record in
MAX and sent to the customer in AZ MVD Now.
2. Customer: The customer signs in to AZ MVD Now, locates the notification, and electronically signs
the document via DocuSign, submitting it back to MVD.
3. MVD: A notification is sent in MAX indicating the document has been signed. The signed document
can now be viewed in MAX.
Step 1 Details: MVD
Step 2 Details: Customer
Step 3 Details: MVD

## Internal Office Notifications

`Cross_Functional/Internal_Office_Notifications.htm`

You are here: Cross Functional > Internal Office Notifications
Internal Office Notifications
Individuals in certain roles, such as supervisors or managers, can use MAX to send notifications to team
members. These are not meant to replace email or other sources of communication. They are designed
to provide a quick, simple way to communicate to teams or individuals who are logged into MAX. There
are two types of notifications: Alerts and In-App Messages. Notifications are brief messages (limited to
256 characters) that expire after a specified amount of time.
Alerts only appear on the CSR's screens if they are logged in to MAX. Alerts pop-up on top of
any existing activity CSRs are working on in MAX, without impacting what the CSR is doing. The
alert appears once and disappears when the alert box is closed. Alerts are good if you need to
communicate about a topic happening in real time, for example:
Alert to Team : "Our Huddle will start at 7:45 instead of 7:30 today".
Alert to Individual: "Sarah, when you are done with this customer, go to lunch".
In-App Messages appear in the Notification Center in MAX. The message will stay in the
Notification Center until the CSR deletes it or the message has expired. For example, if a
supervisor needs to let their Title & Registration team know about a new T&R policy, they can
send an in-app message through MAX that will appears in each CSR's Notification Center.

## Notes Management

`Cross_Functional/Notes_Management.htm`

You are here: Cross Functional > Notes Management
Notes Management
Comments don't exist in MAX the way they did in the legacy system. MAX is designed to automatically
save a history of all services, activities, and events related to the customer. Because a customer's
Service History is systematically recorded, including what was done, when, and by whom, the need to
add comments about customer services has been significantly reduced.
MAX users should always consider Service History as the main resource to research or review
previous account activities. MAX users will see an optional Notes field included in many services,
functions, and on the vehicle record. The optional Notes should be used only when supplemental details
that are not already saved by MAX need to be recorded and attached to the customer record and
history.
Notes should be used very judiciously, with these guidelines:
Using Notes should be the exception, not the rule, because MAX records all activity related to
services (what, when, why, and who)
Notes are captured in the database and as such are part of the record
Think carefully before you add a Note. It can become part of a legal case
Keep Notes thorough, but brief. They have a maximum length of 300 characters
Don't use acronyms, abbreviations, or codes in Notes
Don't state the obvious, especially if the info is already being captured by MAX
Notes are not formatted or edited like a word processor- what you enter is what you get
Ask yourself, is my Note adding value beyond just me? If the answer is no, then a Note should
not be saved
Viewing Notes
Adding Notes During a Service
Adding Notes on Vehicle Record

One Time Mailing Address
Adding a one time mailing address enables you to provide an address where a document should be
mailed but does not change the address on the customer's record. The one time mailing address will
only be used to mail the document to the customer and will not be saved to the customer record. It will

The One-Time Mailing Address (if any) is the first address that MAX checks when mailing items

mailed to (Domicile, Mailing, or Residential/Commercial), use the One-Time Mailing Address to
ensure the correct location for receipt of the items. See Central Issuance and Mailing for more

## Scanning In MAX

`Cross_Functional/Scanning.htm`

You are here: Documents and Scanning > Scanning In MAX
Scanning In MAX
There are multiple ways to scan / upload documents during a service, and how you scan impacts:
Where the documents are saved
If a customer can see the documents in AZ MVD Now
If MAX will automatically use the documents to satisfy future requirements
There are several different options for scanning service-related documents into MAX:
Scan During a Service (for credentials)
Done during the service on the Requirements tab and all scanned documents are saved as
part of the Service Artifacts
Service Finalized (for title and registration)
Done in the Service Finalized screen to scan Title Summary and supporting documentation
Scan to Completed Service
Done when the service has been completed and documents were not 'Scan During a
Service'
Scan Later
Rarely used; Done when scanners are not functionally properly while service customers
Documents scanned to a T & R Service (viewable in Service History) will NOT display in the
Documents area of the Customer Record for security and privacy purposes, because all
Customer Documents are viewable to the Customer in AZ MVD Now.
Documents scanned to a Credential Service (viewable in Service History) will display in the
Documents area of the Customer Record for security and privacy purposes, because all
Customer Documents are viewable to the Customer in AZ MVD Now.
View the Document Management topic for more information about Document Requirements and
locating or viewing documents in MAX.

## Adding a Customer (Individual or Organization)

`Customer/Adding_a_Customer.htm`

You are here: Customer > Adding a Customer (Individual or Organization)
Adding a Customer (Individual or Organization)
An official MVD Customer Record is required for any person or organization seeking services with MVD. If a
customer does not already have a Customer Record, you will need to add a Customer (Individual or
Organization) in MAX.
To avoid creating a duplicate record, be sure to execute at least two searches for the customer prior
to adding them as a new customer.
If using Quick Search, do not use hyphens or dashes when searching for SSNs, EINs, or DOBs.
Special characters can be used for names, just not unique identifiers such as SSN.
If adding a Trust, see Adding a Trust for detailed steps.
1. When no results are found, or the customer is not found in the Search results, click Add New Customer.
2. From the Customer Type dropdown, select Individual to add an Individual customer, or Organization to
add an Organization (Business, Estate, Trust, etc.).

## Adding a Trust

`Customer/Adding_A_Trust.htm`

You are here: Customer > Adding a Trust
Adding a Trust
A Trust should be added as an Organization in MAX where the Trustees would be Individuals.
If needed, you can add DBAs to the Organization record for the Trust to reflect the Trustees on vehicle titling.
If the Trust needs to conduct business through AZ MVD Now, view the Managing AZ MVD Now
Logins for Organizations topic, as they will need to complete an AZ MVD Now Administrator
Application.
View the DBA (Doing Business As) topic or more information. View the Title and Registration topic > Title and
Registration section for more information on Trusts or Ownership Priority, Role Types, & Legal Statuses topic
> Regular Role Types section for more information on legal statuses and Mail Stars.
Creating an Organization Record for a Trust
Mr. Schmidt has set up a Living Trust and would like to title the vehicle in the name of the Trust.
1. Search for the Trust in MAX. View Finding a Customer and Advanced Search - Other for more
information.
2. When the Trust is not found, click Add New Customer.
3. In the Customer dropdown, select Organization.
4. Type the Organization Name of the Trust.
5. In the Organization Type dropdown, select Trust. You must select the correct Organization Type here.
Currently there is no way to change it after the new record is created.
6. Type the EIN of the Trust, or click Generate OIN.
7. Click Add New Address to add the address for the Trust (and to make the Trust a Level 3 customer with
all of the above information. View Level 3 Customer for more information).
8. Click Save.

9. The new Organization Customer Record will display for the Trust.
10. The briefcase indicates this Trust is a Level 3 Customer.
11. To add the Trustee as a DBA (for vehicle titling), click Add Name, and type the name of the Trustee for
the Trust as the DBA. View DBA (Doing Business As) for more information.
12. The Organization Customer Record will now include the DBA name for the Trustee. You may now Title
vehicles in the name of the Trust using the appropriate DBA. View DBA (Doing Business As) for more
information.

## Address Confidentiality Program (ACP)

`Customer/Address_Confidentiality_Program_(ACP).htm`

You are here: Customer > Address Confidentiality Program (ACP)
Address Confidentiality Program (ACP)
Eligible customers may apply to have certain information redacted (hidden) from their MVD records to
ensure it is not publicly available. Redacting a customer's personal information is done by adding a
characteristic to their customer record.
If a customer's information is hidden, do not make any attempt to view their confidential data unless it
is necessary to conduct Division business on their behalf.
CSRs have the ability to add or remove this Characteristic on the customer's record. CSR must
write down the ACP start date and enter that same date into MAX when they are adding the
ACP Characteristic back on the record.
If any issues, please contact Madelene Carbajal. DO NOT contact the Secretary of State
Address Confidentiality Program.
The ACP will allow a person who has been subjected to domestic violence, sexual offense, or stalking to
keep their residence address confidential and not accessible to the general public.
The CSR will enter the ACP customer's actual resident address as the residential address in
MAX and the ACP substitute address with registered apartment number as the mailing address.
ACP customers will ONLY need to provide proof of mailing address not residential for credential
issuance.
 
At no time will any state or local government entity intentionally or knowingly release the ACP's
participant’s confidential address information.
The information is protected under the Arizona Law and the entity could be found guilty of a
Class 1 misdemeanor.

## Agent and Customer

`Customer/Agent_and_Customer.htm`

You are here: Customer > Agent and Customer
Agent and Customer
In MAX the person who pays for a service is called an Agent. There are some important things to understand
about the role of an Agent:
An Agent is the person who is actually paying, and accepting financial responsibility, for the MVD
service.
In most cases, the Customer of a MAX service is also the Agent.
Separating the Agent and the Customer roles in MAX allows the system to apply any potential fees or
penalties to the correct record if there is a problem processing the payment.
Separating the Agent and the Customer roles also allows for improved verification of vehicle transfer
claims.
The Dashboard will display the Agent and the Customer names for services in progress or queued.

## Characteristics

`Customer/Characteristics.htm`

You are here: Customer > Characteristics
Characteristics
Characteristics are associated to a qualified customer and indicate and control things such as:
Eligibility for a special plate
Eligibility for a fee exemption (view Fee Exemptions for more information)
Printing a designation on their credential (i.e. emancipated minor or Veteran)
A need to have their customer record redacted (i.e. Address Confidentiality Program
Characteristics)
When a fee exemption is to be used during a service, the Characteristic does not have to be
entered prior to the service. By adding the exemption, the Characteristic will automatically be
added after the service is completed.
Characteristics are added either through the customer record or through a service where the
characteristic needs to be utilized.
Some characteristics are restricted as to who can add, edit and remove the characteristic
from the customer record. Please check the Characteristics (below) for details.
Characteristics for Organization Customers may also control some of the things listed above, but
generally are used to control access to interfaces and AZ MVD Now where they have unique services
available based on the characteristic. For example:
An organization with a Dealer characteristic will be able to access the Dealer Suite of Services
Professional Driving Schools will be able to update Test Results via AZ MVD Now with the
appropriate characteristic applied to the Organization Record

## Customer Record and Customer Account Overview

`Customer/Customer_Record_Overview.htm`

You are here: Customer > Customer Record Overview
Customer Record and Customer Account Overview
A MAX Customer Record or official MVD Customer Record is required for any person or organization seeking
services with us. The Customer Record is made up of important information about the customer, such as
identification types, contact information, characteristics, etc. To help organize customers, two types of
customer records exist in MAX:
Individual Customer Record - An Individual Customer is any person who is doing business with the
MVD, such as obtaining a driver's license or registering a vehicle.
Organization Customer Record - An Organization Customer is a business or organized group with a
particular purpose, such as a Dealer, Trust, Financial Institution, etc.

## Financial Receipt History

`Customer/Financial_Receipt_History.htm`

You are here: Finance & Cash Management > Financial Receipt History
Financial Receipt History
The Financial Receipt History (also call Account Service History) is a collection of Payment Receipts
that impact a Customer's Account.
Because Authorized Third Party (ATP) offices are always the Agent for every service, their ATP
Customer Account will include every Payment Receipt processed by that Third Party, whether it
impacts the balance of the Customer Account or not.
The Financial Receipt History will display the following information:
Receipt Number: Click to download a PDF of the Receipt
Business Transaction Number: Also known as the Service ID on a Payment Receipt
Service Date: The date the service was completed
Business Unit: The location where the service was completed. If the customer completed a
service through AZ MVD Now, that will display here
Customer: The Customer receiving the benefit of the service
Service: The name of the Service in MAX that was completed for the customer
Amount: The total dollar amount for the associated service. This amount may or may not impact
the Account Balance
Balance: The running Account Balance. This figure will raise or lower if the service resulted in a
debit or a credit to the account
Searching Financial Receipt History
ATP: Searching a Specific Receipt Number (TRP Converted to Full Title and
Reg)
ATP: Searching a Specific Receipt Number (Cancel Service Credit)

## Law Enforcement (LECU) Redactions

`Customer/Law_Enforcement_(LECU)_Redactions.htm`

You are here: Customer > Law Enforcement (LECU) Redactions
Law Enforcement (LECU) Redactions
Eligible customers may apply to have certain information redacted (hidden) from their MVD records to
ensure it is not publicly available. Redacting a customer's personal information is done by adding a
characteristic to their customer record.
If a customer's information is hidden, do not make any attempt to view their confidential data unless it
is necessary to conduct Division business on their behalf.
LECU Redaction Eligibility
An eligible person, as defined in statute, may petition the court to prevent their identifying information
from being disclosed to any person except as provided by law.
Once approved by the courts, a certified court order will be sent to the Office of Inspector General (OIG)
and within 150 days, the Division is required to redact the customer's record.
For other LECU redactions, if the Characteristic needs to be removed to verify customer
identity, contact OIG/LECU (Law Enforcement Coordination Unit) before proceeding with the
service.
If you have begun the service, but have not finalized, delete the service and start again after
OIG/LECU has lifted the redaction.
If you forgot to call OIG/LECU, have finalized the service in MAX, and notice the artifacts are
printing with the incorrect address, see Title & Registration on Redacted Record.
Redacted Information for LECU Redactions
Home, Mailing, and Domicile Addresses, including City, State, ZIP, County, & Country
Mobile, FAX, Home & Business Phone Numbers
Email Addresses
SSN
DOB
Eye and hair color; weight and height
Electronic Signature
Photo

Processing a LECU Redacted Record
Contact  LECU at (602) 712-7488 or email at LECU@azdot.gov to have LECU temporarily remove the
redaction so the service can be completed.
DO NOT send any customer to OIG or give out OIG's address.
Once you have processed the service(s), you must contact OIG/LECU after the customer has
verified that all information on the credential and/or title and registration documents is correct so
the redaction can be put back on.
LECU Redaction: Address Change or Duplicate Credential Requested
Any address changes, or if a duplicate credential is requested in-person or by phone may be processed
by the CSR as long as the redaction was lifted by LECU prior to starting the service .
The redaction must be added back onto the record by LECU after the service was completed.
LECU Redaction: Processing T & R Transactions
Before starting a T & R service for a customer with a LECU Redaction verify with the customer that their
address has not changed since their last service with MVD or an ATP.
If their address has changed then contact LECU to have the redaction lifted
If their address has not changed then the following T & R transaction do not require the LECU
Redaction to be lifted:
Initially titling and/or registering a vehicle into the redacted Individual's name in MAX
Registration Renewals
Transferring a vehicle out of the redacted individual's name (this transaction never requires
the LECU redaction to be lifted)
The customer's full address will print on the service artifacts even with the redaction on their
record.
Always have the customer verify their address is correct. If the address in not correct then
follow the steps in T and R Scenarios topic > Title & Registration on Redacted Record section.
LECU Redaction: AZ MVD Now Services
Customers with a LECU redacted characteristic do not have the ability to active or utilize an AZ MVD
Now account without having the redaction lifted first. This decision was made for security reasons by
OIG.
A customer with a LECU Redaction may utilize the Express Services on AZ MVD Now when
they select 'Continue as Guest' without lifting their redaction. Their information will display as
redacted.

For other types of redactions, view the following topics:
Address Confidentiality Program (ACP)
Orders of Protection or Injunction Against Harassment (OOP/IAH)
Policies
4.2.4 Record Redaction
Forms
#40-5122 Travel License/Identification Application
#40-5122s Travel License/Identification Application (Spanish)

## Logging Into MAX

`Customer/Logging_Into_MAX.htm`

You are here: MAX Basics > Logging Into MAX
Logging Into MAX
To log into MAX:
From your browser (use Chrome), navigate to the MAX site: https://web.mvm.azdot.gov
1. Click Login in the MAX Navigation Bar to be directed to Microsoft online to enter your credentials.
2. Type your assigned User Name if not already saved. Your user name will likely be
FirstInitialLastName@mvm.azdot.gov (example: scooper@mvm.azdot.gov). It should match your
AZDOT email name (i.e. jsmith or jsmith1).
3. Click Next.
4. Type your Password (provided to you separately).
5. Click Sign in.

6. Click Yes to stay signed in.
You will know you have successfully logged in when your name appears where the Login prompt was
previously.
If you encounter issues with your password, contact the ADOT Service Desk at 602-712-7249 for
assistance. Clicking the Forgot Password or Reset Password links on the Sign-In screen will NOT
work.

Password Requirements
Characters allowed:
A – Z
a - z
0 – 9
@ # $ % ^ & * - _ ! + = [ ] { } | \ : ' , . ? / ` ~ " ( ) ; < >
blank space
Characters not allowed: Unicode characters.
Password restrictions:
A minimum of 8 characters and a maximum of 256 characters.
Requires three out of four of the following:
................Lowercase characters.
................Uppercase characters.
................Numbers (0-9).
................Symbols (see the previous password restrictions).
Password expiry duration: (Maximum password age) 90 days.
Password change history: The last password can't be used again when the user changes a
password.
Password reset history: The last password can be used again when the user resets a forgotten
password.
 
Selecting a Business Unit
A dropdown will appear for you to select the Business Unit you wish to work from. A Business Unit is similar
to selecting the "office" when you log into the legacy system. In MAX, it will help define what work you will be
doing (i.e. Dealer Licensing versus Globe Office) and the associated cash management for that location.
1. From the Business Unit dropdown, select your Business Unit.
2. Click Confirm.

Changing to a different Business Unit
On occasion, you may need to login to a different Business Unit.
Example: As a CSR, you spend a day at a different MVD Field Office. You will need to log in to the
correct Business Unit in order to accept the Cash Drawer and transact business for that Unit in MAX.
1. From the Navigation bar, click your Profile (User Name).
2. Click Business Unit.
3. MAX will display the Business Unit Selection screen as shown above. Select the different Business Unit
and click Confirm.

## Request Customer Disbursement

`Customer/Request_Customer_Disbursement.htm`

Request Customer Disbursement
If a customer has an unused balance on a Prepaid Voucher or a balance in their Customer Account,
those funds can be returned to them using the Disbursement Request feature.
The customer has the option of either receiving a check, or to have the disbursement credited to their
bank account (this option only available when submitted by customer).
Only authorized Organizations can request a Disbursement via AZ MVD Now. "Authorized" includes the
following:
Has the Trusted Organization characteristic on their Customer Record. See Trusted Organization
for more information.
Has the Limited Trust Organization characteristic on their Customer Record.
Has an active Fleet associated to their record.
Check disbursements are usually received within 7-10 days.
A Disbursement Request can only be made 7 business days after the original account funding
as an anti-fraud measure. If the request is made within 7 days of account funding, you will get
an error. This does not apply to Registration Credits or other MVD refunds. Disbursement
requests for those funds can be made immediately.
If the customer funded their own account, and had no MVD Refunds or Credits, they will only
be able to make one Disbursement Request in a 30-day period. Additional Disbursement
Requests from the account may be made for each MVD Refund or Credit to the account.
Example: a customer received 6 plate credits in one month, so MAX will allow 7
Disbursement Requests (Number of MVD Refunds/Credits + 1)
If the customer contacts the MVD as to why they have not received their disbursement checks that have
already been processed, the customer or CSR can email the Refund Unit at
ADOT_PFTO_Refunds@azdot.gov (preferred method of contact) to inquire, or call at 602-712-7507.
Requesting a Customer Disbursement for the Customer in MAX

## Voter Registration - Standalone Service

`Customer/Standalone_Voter_Registration.htm`

You are here: Customer > Voter Registration - Standalone Service
Voter Registration - Standalone Service
The Standalone Voter Registration service in MAX is available to allow a customer to register to vote without
having a credential processed or by completing a Voter Registration form.
The customer will be completing their preferences using a tablet
This service is only available in FOOF Business Units
This service will follow the same rules for eligibility to vote
Processing Standalone Voter Registration
1. From the Services menu, select Voter Registration under Miscellaneous.
2. Search for the customer.
The customer must have an established record with authorized presence
3. Click Add.
4. Click Prompt Customer Questions.
This will send the questions to the tablet for the customer to answer
Customer on the Tablet
5. Select English or Spanish, as applicable.
6. Click Yes to register to vote.
7.  Select Party Affiliation.

8. If Other is selected for Party Affiliation, a onscreen keyboard will display. Type the Other Party name.
9.  Click Yes or No to active early voting question.
10. Click Submit Questions.
This will send the answers back to the CSR's screen

CSR in MAX
11. If Other was selected, verify the Party Affiliation was typed in. If it wasn't, type the proper party per the
customer's verbal request.
12. Click Confirm.
13. Verify the Service Summary screen with the customer.
If incorrect, click Reopen and edit as needed
True = Yes
False = No
14. Click Continue.

15. Click Pay Now.
The Checkout - Service Summary screen may display for a short time
16. In the Service Finalized screen, print the Voter Registration Receipt and provide to the customer.
If customer requests, print and provide Payment Receipt

## Trusted Organization

`Customer/Trusted_Organization.htm`

You are here: Customer > Trusted Organization
Trusted Organization
Certain organizations that do business with MVD need to have the ability to fund their accounts and/or
request disbursements that are beyond the pre-set limits. Once an organization is determined to be a
Trusted Organization, the Trusted Organization Characteristic will be added to their customer record.
That characteristic allows the organization:
No limit on the amount or frequency of funds added to their account
No limit on the amount or frequency of the disbursement requests
The ability to request Digital Disbursements from their Organization Customer Account
Only the Director of MVD is authorized to approve the Trusted Organization Characteristic.
The Trusted Organization Characteristic can only be added to Organization Records in MAX by
the Account Support Unit (all organizations) and the Dealer Licensing Unit (Dealers only).
View Characteristics for details on how a characteristic is added.

# Driver


## 12 Year Photo Update

`Driver/Credential_Issuance/12_Year_Photo_Update.htm`

You are here: Driver & Event Management > Credential Issuance > 12 Year Photo
Update
12 Year Photo Update
Arizona issues an extended Non Travel driver license that does not expire until age 65, but residents
are required to update their photo every 12 years. At the end of 12 years, a notice will be sent
requesting them to visit a MVD Office or Authorized Third Party for a new photo and replacement driver
license.
Licensees will be given until the first day of the month following their birth month to act in accordance
with the required photo update.
Customers needing a 12 Year Photo Update must apply in person and have a new photo taken. Failing
to update photo may cause the customer to be ineligible to receive the following driver services:
Online services on AZ MVD Now
Driver license and instruction permit issuance
Collection fees, excluding Abandoned Vehicle Fees, Non-Sufficient Funds, and Financial
Responsibility (Mandatory Insurance Actions or Financial Responsibility topic and/or Vehicle
Registration Suspension Fees)
Processing the 12 Year Photo Update
MAX requires a new photo be taken for any credential service. MAX will restrict your ability to complete
a credential service until a new photo is in place.
In MAX, use the Credential Issuance service and select Duplicate. If the customer's credential is
recently expired or will expire soon, use the Credential Renewal service.
Customers that have both Non-Travel and an Arizona Travel credentials are required to have both
credentials updated. This will ensure issue dates and photos are the same on both credentials.
Customers are required to pay for all applicable fees associated with the issuance of both credentials.
Exceptions to online Duplicate Credential requests is a customer who is required to register as
a sex offender, or a customer requiring a 12 Year Photo Update, they must apply in person and
have a new photo taken.

## Acceptable Documentation for Non-Commercial Credentials

`Driver/Credential_Issuance/Acceptable_Documentation_for_Credential.htm`

You are here: Driver & Event Management > Credential Issuance > Acceptable
Documentation for Non-Commercial Credentials
Acceptable Documentation for Non-Commercial Credentials
Acceptable Documents
Primary forms of identification are used to establish the customer’s name, DOB, and authorized
presence in the U.S. Primary documentation provided must be listed on the primary identification list
and contain the full name and DOB. In addition to at least one document from the primary identification
list, the customer must provide their full social security number for verification purposes.
Original Credentials have a 10-year retention period (from date of issuance) for all source
documents provided to establish a name and date of birth, including the Credential Application. All
source documents must be scanned within the service when issuing:
Original Identification Card or Driver License
Original Travel Credential
Original Instruction Permit
A Credential Renewal with a 'Pre 96' characteristic, view Authorized Presence topic > Pre
96 Characteristic section for more information
One Primary form of identification, view the Acceptable Primary Documents section for a listing of
acceptable primary forms of identification
Social Security Number written by the customer on their application, view Policy 7.1.8 Social
Security Online Verification (SSOLV) for more information
The last four numbers of the SSN on the application is acceptable for existing
customers or system generated applications (verbal confirmation may be required)
The full SSN is required on the application for new customers that fill out the application
by hand (not system generated)
The Division will not issue a credential when the customer presents documentation that does not
appear to be legitimate or acceptable. View the Fraudulent Documents or Identity topic for more
information if documentation does not appear to be legitimate.

Foreign applicants are required to provide original documents when requesting an original,
reinstatement or renewal of any credential type. Credentials issued to foreign applicants will
expire according to the documents presented for authorized presence.
Foreign applicants applying for an Arizona credential should be assisted by a foreign
documents expert.

Acceptable Proof of Address Documents
Unacceptable Documents

## Authorized Presence

`Driver/Credential_Issuance/Authorized Presence/Authorized_Presence.htm`

You are here: Driver & Event Management > Credential Issuance > Authorized
Presence > Authorized Presence
Authorized Presence
Authorized Presence requires a customer to prove eligibility to be in the United States as authorized
under Federal Law. All individuals applying for an Arizona credential must provide proof of Authorized
Presence when apply for:
A renewal of a limited Arizona Driver License
An original Arizona Travel or Non-Travel Driver License, ID, or Permit
If you are processing a foreign credential and have NOT participated in Fraudulent
Documentation Recognition training, you must have a supervisor or manager review the
documents prior to finalizing the service to confirm the documents are valid.
Proving Authorized Presence
Systematic Alien Verification for Entitlements (SAVE) System Verification
Types of Authorized Presence
There are two primary categories of Authorized Presence and they are broken down into five options in
MAX.
Non-Foreign:
US Citizen / Nonforeign: Were born in the United States, District of Columbia, Puerto Rico,
Guam, Northern Mariana Islands, or Virgin Islands, or born in an outlying possession of the U.S.
(American Samoa or Swain’s Island) on or after the date the U.S. acquired the possession.
Naturalized Citizen: Individuals granted citizenship status by the U.S. Citizenship and
Immigration Services OR foreign-born children, under age 18, residing in the U.S. with their birth
or adoptive parents, at least one of whom is a U.S. citizen by birth or naturalization
View the U.S. Citizen - Nonforeign Authorized Presence topic for more details.
Foreign:
Resident/Permanent Alien: A Permanent Resident (I-551 or PRC) is a lawful or conditional
immigrant residing permanently in the United States (US) with the authorization of U.S.
Citizenship and immigration Services (USCIS)

Temporary INS: USCIS issues the Form I-766 to a specific classes of aliens as evidence of their
employment authorization in the U.S. They include but are not limited to, applicants for
adjustment of status, refugee/asylee, individuals with Temporary Protected Status, individuals
granted deferred action, including Childhood Arrivals, and F1 students who have completed their
studies and are approved for Optional Practical Training
Canada: Were born in Canada and retain Canadian citizenship
View the Foreign Authorized Presence topic for more details.
Viewing Authorized Presence History

## AZ Privilege Status

`Driver/Credential_Issuance/AZ_Privilege_Status.htm`

You are here: Driver & Event Management > Credential Issuance > AZ Privilege Status
AZ Privilege Status
When viewing a Driver Record or when providing a Credential Service, one of the first things you will see is
the AZ Privilege Status, which is a quick visual indicator for the following:
Credentials a customer has (commercial, non commercial, identification)
Credentials a customer is not legally excluded from obtaining
Credentials a customer may obtain after satisfying some sort of compliance
Credentials a customer is not eligible to obtain due to withdrawal or death
The AZ Privilege Status feature is designed to give you an immediate quick reference, so you can readily set
proper expectations with your customer. If you see a caution or a stop icon, you know the customer may not
be able to get the credential they are seeking before you begin the service.
In addition to Privilege Status, this section provides additional helpful information:
1. State of Record Pointer - indicates whether or not Arizona is the state of record for each of the pointer
types. Yes indicates Arizona is the state of record, No indicates Arizona is not the state of record.
2. AZ Privilege Status - a quick visual indicator of what credentials the customer has and what they are or
aren't eligible to obtain. See details below.
3. Earliest Eligibility Date - for a customer with a withdrawal that impacts their credential status, but they
are eligible to get the privilege back in the future, this indicates the earliest date they may qualify to get
their full privileges back.
4. DL Earliest Restricted Eligibility Date - If the customer has a withdrawal that impacts their credential
status, but they may be eligible to obtain a restricted credential in the future, this indicates the earliest
date they may qualify. If "Not Eligible" is present it means it doesn't apply or is not an option for this
customer.
5. Required Hard Time - for withdrawals that require a waiting period before the customer is eligible to
reapply for a restricted credential, this reflects the duration of the waiting period (e.g., for the first Admin
Per Se offense and the customer chooses not to apply for a SIIRDL, the customer serves 30 days of hard
time before they are eligible for 60 days of restricted driving). If "Not Eligible" is present it means it doesn't
apply or is not an option for this customer.

 Definitions and Examples
