## Update Registration

`T_&_R/Title_&_Registration/Update_Registration.htm`

You are here: Title & Registration Services > Update Registration
Update Registration
Update Registration is used most often for:
Replacing a plate (lost, stolen, damaged, or new plate)
Requesting a special or personalized plate for the first time; title or registration is already
established. View the Personalized and Special Plates topic for information on how to process
Changing the vehicle use (e.g., regular to commercial)
Changing the declared gross weight
Changing the vehicle's domicile address
Replacing a decal (tab) (lost, stolen or damaged) in addition to other changes above
Add or remove a fee exemption
The Update Registration service will charge all appropriate fees for the changes you are making (e.g.
changing plates). If you have made an error in a previous T&R Service, use the T and R Corrections
service so that MAX appropriately adjusts and credits fees.
You cannot use Update Registration if the registration has expired. Use Renew
Registration and make changes during that service.
If the vehicle has a Title Only or ARO, use the Title & Reg service and a prompt will
display asking 'Do you wish to add a registration?'
IMPORTANT: When doing ANY Update Registration service, it will automatically
send out updated registration documents/tabs. DO NOT do a no-fee duplicate tab,
or the customer will get two of them.
Replacement Plate
When a customer requests a new plate that has been lost, stolen or damaged, the request may be done
in office or through AZ MVD Now, using the Update Registration services.
Once processed, the plate, registration, and decal will be mailed to them.
Requirements for Requesting a Replacement in Office
The vehicle registration must be current
A new plate number will issued for a lost or stolen plate

If the plate is personalized, a new personalization will be required. A $5 Replacement Plate
Fee, plus postage, will apply
Can be requested by the registered owner, family member (with the same last name and address
as the registered owner), or by using a POA from the registered owner
Pays $5.00* Replacement Plate Fee, plus postage
This fee can be waived provided the owner has not moved, still maintains the mailing
address of record and claims non-delivery by the United States Postal Department (e.g.,
renewed by mail, or AZ MVD Now, etc.). The claim of non-delivery must be made 10 days
after the issue date, but within 45 days of the issue date to qualify for a no-fee
replacement
View the Renew Registration topic > Replace Plate or Replace Plate - Lost/Stolen section for steps on
how to replace a plate.
*If a customer pays the $50 reinstatement fee then the $5 lost plate fee is waived. Postage will
be charged.
If the $50 reinstatement fee is voided then the customer shall pay the $5 lost plate fee when the
plate has been confiscated.
If a Replacement Registration and Decal are needed (but not a plate), view the Tab
Replacement topic.
Updating Registration
Add Domicile Address
Replacing the Tab

Vehicle Domicile Address
The vehicle domicile address is used to indicate where the vehicle is housed or stored, if different from
the customer's residential address (e.g. storage yard, or winter cabin, etc.).
Where a vehicle is stored / driven may impact emissions requirements.
The Adding a Vehicle Domicile Address During a Service service is available during any registration
service or in the Vehicle Information Page when not in a service.
Inform the customer that when the vehicle is no longer located in a domicile area they will need
to update the domicile address in an MVD office. Customers are not able to change the
domicile address in AZ MVD Now.

## Vehicle MVR

`T_&_R/Title_&_Registration/Vehicle_MVR.htm`

You are here: Title & Registration Services > Vehicle Management > Vehicle MVR
Vehicle MVR
A vehicle Motor Vehicle Record (MVR) is the printout providing the history of titles, vehicle permits, and
lienholders.
The Federal Driver's Privacy Protection Act (DPPA) regulates the manner in which the Division may
release information from its driver license or motor vehicle records. Anyone who knowingly obtains,
discloses or uses personal information from a motor vehicle record for a use not permitted is subject to
civil and/or criminal penalties.
In addition, anyone who misrepresents their identity or makes a false statement with the intent to obtain
such information in a manner not authorized by law is also subject to civil and/or criminal penalties.
The Central Issuance Mailing Dashboard > Processing Dates tab provides the current date
mailed in MVRs are being worked.

## Watch Your Car

`T_&_R/Title_&_Registration/Watch_Your_Car.htm`

You are here: Title & Registration Services > Vehicle Management > Watch Your Car
Watch Your Car
The Watch Your Car decal program is a free, voluntary program whereby Arizona vehicle owners can
enroll their vehicle(s) with the Arizona Automobile Theft Authority. Participants receive decals for their
front and rear windows. By displaying the decals, vehicle owners convey to law enforcement that their
vehicle is not usually driven between 1:00 AM and 5:00 AM, when the majority of auto thefts occur.
If a police officer witnesses the vehicle in operation between these hours, they have the right to stop the
vehicle and verify it is being legally operated by the rightful owner.
By enrolling in the Watch Your Car Program, vehicle owners also authorize law enforcement officials to
stop their vehicle at any time during the day or night within one mile of the border, if there is a suspicion
the vehicle is being illegally operated.
When the customer elects to transfer ownership of the vehicle, they will be responsible for removing the
stickers and informing the Division or the Arizona Automobile Theft Authority of their cancellation in the
program.
Decals and a cancellation form for future use will be mailed to the customer within 2-4 weeks of
enrolling.
Enrolling a Customer in Watch Your Car

## Assign State VIN

`T_&_R/Vehicle_Management/Assign_State_VIN.htm`

You are here: Title & Registration Services > Vehicle Management > Assign State VIN
Assign State VIN
An Arizona Serial Number (AZ SNO), or AZ Number, is issued to homemade trailers (and other specially
constructed or reconstructed vehicles) when there is no VIN, or when the assigned AZ Number,
manufacturer's serial number or other identifying numbers have been altered, removed, obliterated,
defaced, or omitted.
The Arizona Serial Number (SNO) decals will stay in offices (not central issuance).
Arizona Serial Number (AZ SNO or AZ Number)
The Arizona Serial Number:
Is a self-adhesive, serial, or identification decal
Consists of the characters AZ followed by six numerals and a control number
Becomes the VIN for the vehicle OR trailer and on all paperwork used for future vehicle services
Cost is $5.00
The $5.00 fee* will need to be collected after the service is completed using the Miscellaneous
Purchase service and selecting State Assigned VIN Fee.
*ONLY for homemade vehicles/trailers, this does not include VINs with less than 17 characters
using the State Assigned VIN Type.
When an AZ Serial Number is assigned, a Special Serial Number Application, form #48-5108 must be
completed. The application:
Is filled out by the customer and must make a statement as to how they built the trailer
Is completed and signed by the inspector (at time of inspection) OR
Is completed and signed by the CSR (when the inspector did not complete the application)

When an AZ Serial Number is required to be affixed to a vehicle or trailer, always make sure the
vehicle or trailer is still at the office PRIOR to processing the service.
The vehicle or trailer will need to be added to MAX during the subsequent Title & Registration
service. View the Add a Vehicle, or Title and Registration services for more information.
Completing the Special Serial Number Application
Affixing the AZ Serial Number
Homemade Vehicles or Trailers
A CSR certified in inspections may affix the decal, as only a Level I Inspection is required.
Manufactured Vehicles, Trailers , or Mobile Homes
Vehicles, trailers, or mobile homes that had a manufacturer's serial/ID number, or an other identifying
number that has been altered, removed, obliterated, defaced, or omitted, will have an Enforcement
(ECD) Officer affix the decal, as a Level II/III Inspection is required.
The customer never attaches the decal to the vehicle or trailer.
Policies

## Searching for a Vehicle

`T_&_R/Vehicle_Management/Search_for_Vehicle.htm`

You are here: Title & Registration Services > Vehicle Management > Searching for a Vehicle
Searching for a Vehicle
Search for a vehicle using the Quick Search field at the top of the MAX navigation bar. You can search by:
VIN
Short VIN (6 digits)
Plate number
Registration Number
Title
The Quick Search automatically applies an Exact Match function to Plates, full VIN and Title. If
wanting to search for a partial plate number, use Advanced Search to remove the Exact Match
function.
See Advanced Search - Other for additional search options related to vehicles:
Plates
Permit
VIN
Title
Registration
1. Change the search type to Vehicle.
2. Type the vehicle's unique identifier .
3. Click Searchor press Enter on the keyboard.
If the unique identifier relates to only one vehicle (e.g. a complete VIN or Exact Match on a Plate), the Vehicle
Information page opens immediately. If it does not open immediately, go to Step 4.

4. If the identifier is associated to multiple vehicles (for example, a partial VIN) a list of search results
displays. Click the VIN hyperlink to view the Vehicle Information Page.
The Vehicle Information Page opens.

If your search criteria is too broad, you may get the warning "Too many records found, enter
additional data to narrow the search". Refine your search (e.g. using full VIN instead of short VIN) or
consider using Advanced Search.

# TRPs


## Cancel TRP

`TRPs/Cancel_TRP.htm`

You are here: Title & Registration Services > TRPs > Cancel TRP
Cancel TRP
When a Recreate TRP service was completed in error, or if the interface with the Service Arizona Dealer
Suite failed when a Dealer canceled the TRP, you will use a Cancel TRP service.
The only Business Units that have access to the Cancel TRP service are AADA and the Dealer
Licensing Unit.
If you are not in these Business Units and need a TRP canceled, you must submit a MAX Support
Form and include the following:
Customer #
VIN
TRP Number
Plate Number (for trade-in vehicle, if applicable)
Request a Cancel TRP service, and indicate a Cancel reason.
For more information regarding TRPs and Credit Accounts in MAX, see Processing TRPs and Registration
Credits on Dealer Sales .
Canceling a Permit
1. From the Services menu, selectCancel TRP.
2. Search for the Customer related to this service.
3. Click the Select icon next to the TRP you need to cancel.

4. Click Add.
5. Select the Cancellation Reason.
6. Click Confirm to ensure all required information is provided and to check eligibility against business rules.
If something related to the service is incorrect or ineligible, alert(s) describing the issue(s) will display. If
everything is correct, you are taken to the Service Summary.

7. In Service Summary, review information for accuracy and click Continue to calculate taxes and fees.

8. This is a no fee service. If you want to continue, click Pay Now to finalize the transaction. If you need to
complete additional services for the customer, click Add to Cart.

9. Download and print the service document(s) and provide  to the customer. Note: If you need to access
the Payment Receipt or any of the service documents in the future, search for the customer record, and
view the customer's Documents. The customer can access these documents in AZ MVD Now.

## Processing TRPs & Registration Credits on Dealer Sales

`TRPs/Registration_Credit_on_Dealer_Sales_(CSR).htm`

You are here: Title & Registration Services > TRPs > Registration Credit on Dealer
Sales (CSR)
Processing TRPs and Registration Credits on Dealer Sales
Overview
When a customer buys a vehicle from a dealership and they trade in a vehicle there are several things
that occur that may impact the Title & Registration on the vehicle they are purchasing.
When a customer purchases a vehicle through a dealership, any available Registration Credit
from the vehicle they are trading in may be used toward the payment of the Title and Registration
of the new vehicle, if available.
The dealer MUST indicate which plate the credit is coming from at the time the TRP is issued. If
the plate is not indicated, the customer will need to work directly with the MVD/ATP to obtain the
Registration Credit.
When the TRP is issued and the plate is indicated in the TRP service, the registration on that
vehicle is canceled in MAX.
When the T & R service is processed on the vehicle with the TRP, the TRP will be "consumed"
and the registration cycle will start the first day of the month following the issue date of the TRP.
In MAX, the Registration Credit is first credited to a “Credit Account” under the customer’s name
and then the funds are transferred to the account of the Dealer or ATP (whoever completes the
service) when the T & R service is completed.
If there is a balance remaining in the Credit Account after the T & R Fees for the new vehicle are
paid, then that balance is transferred to the customer’s Account.
Title and Registration fees for the purchase of the new vehicle are determined by the dealership
at closing and the buyer pays the dealership at that time.
Finding an Existing TRP
Determining if Registration Credit is Available
Title and Registration Service with Registration Credit
Deal is Reversed
